## Pass 119 — F36 `CISA-40` `SA6` Root B two-phase relief and deployable-retention technology program

**Question.** What technology competition, article bank, source-state envelope, measurement system, challenge campaign, selection logic, public authority, workforce, schedule and fiscal commitment are required to discover a physically credible Root B relief and retention architecture for one two-tonne `SA6` source train?

**Result.** Pass 119 creates `RHT-1` and rejects the inherited single 500 mm route as a sufficient planning answer. At the declared cold source state, a fifty-percent available-area screen transfers 2,000 kg in approximately 161.4 seconds against the inherited 120-second terminal target. The result does not certify real DME flow; it is enough to reopen the architecture under the conservative planning law.

Three candidates enter without selection. `RC-1` uses one 600 mm path, a 2,500 kg receiver allocation and 2,200 m³ segmented low-pressure holder. Its cold challenged transfer is approximately 112.1 seconds and its long-horizon holder margin is 769 m³. `RC-2` uses two 450 mm paths, a 6,500 kg receiver, 2.5 MW condenser, 1,200 MJ thermal store and 600 m³ holder. Its screen is approximately 99.7 seconds with 43.5 m³ of holder margin. `RC-3` uses three 350 mm paths, a 5,344 kg receiver, 1.5 MW condenser, 650 MJ thermal store, 1,200 m³ holder and independently authorized 0.25 kg/s terminal oxidizer limited to one percent of source. Its screen is approximately 82.3 seconds with 166.5 m³ holder margin. Planning results create no ranking.

Four declared source states range from 258 K/0.35 MPa absolute with no external heat to 353 K/2.0 MPa absolute with 8 MW of external heat. A sensible-plus-external-energy screen produces vapor fractions from about 5.4 percent to a capped 100 percent. A homogeneous ideal-gas choking expression reduced by a declared 0.15 two-phase factor sizes paths. Neither method qualifies flashing fronts, slip, aerosol entrainment, ice, acoustic coupling, vessel blowdown, line inventory, receiver dynamics or structural response.

Each architecture receives two unlike builders. Each architecture–builder pair receives two scaled routes, two full-scale site trains, two destructive witnesses, one regeneration spare and one public calibration reference: forty-eight articles. Eight challenges cover cold/icing, route-area loss, wrong-side pressure, receiver initial fill, hot full source, fire heat with active safety-energy denial, partial holder plus weather, and site network plus primary-time denial.

Three architectures, eight challenges, two builders, two sibling sites and one challenge/control pair produce 192 paired full-scale events. Seventy-two development events, twenty-four destructive events and eighteen reset or site-loss events produce 114 support events and a 306-event campaign. Paired events consume 240 tonnes of controlled DME source and destructive work consumes 48 tonnes, for 288 tonnes total.

Sixteen measurement planes follow the source from vessel interior to evidence custody. Eighteen functions create 504 channels and approximately 288.736 MB/s of native data. A 300-second full-rate interval, six-hour one-percent tail, two originals and 1.35 overhead produce approximately 402.267 GB per event. The campaign with a thirty-percent reserve requires approximately 160.022 TB, giving a 3.60 capacity ratio inside the inherited 576 TB public vault.

Twelve conjunctive rules govern each candidate. Cold transfer, holder margin, receiver allocation, no-common-path topology, passive first stage, accounted and retained fractions, backpressure, reset, unlike-builder replication, site replication and all eight accepted protocol dispositions must each close. No weighted score can compensate for failed safety, mass-accounting, evidence or replication. The board may select one or more candidates, continue or reject all three.

The forty-two-month program uses 426 positions inside the inherited 448-position establishment. Gross capital is USD 875M and annual support is USD 148M/year, creating USD 1.393B of program exposure. CISA owns USD 530M/90M per year; a shared technology account owns USD 345M/58M per year. Replacing USD 172M/18M per year of inherited Root B content creates a net new CISA increment of USD 358M/72M per year. `BRD-5R` becomes USD 1.298B/246M per year, exceeding the CISA capital boundary by USD 338M while leaving USD 14M/year. `BRD-5U` becomes USD 1.771B/345M per year and exceeds both boundaries. The mismatch remains visible.

The twenty-three-sheet workbook recalculates with zero formula errors. All 910 terminal audits pass. Fifty-three disposable sensitivity tests respond and restore, including individual builder-package, mass-closure, board, rescue and evidence-original authorities. Every sheet was rendered and visually reviewed; two readability defects were corrected before final export. The final workbook is 165,674 bytes with SHA-256 `486bf6786bd69312e49610e0afde6706d8cd079479d01aa22b23cfc1882d947a`. Four hundred eighty-three planning gates pass, 398 physical gates remain open and nine downstream gates remain blocked. Accepted articles, events, protocols and physical sources remain zero.

## Pass 120 — F36 `CISA-40` `SA6` Root B full-scale transient-rig physical specification

**Date:** 9 September 2026

**Question.** What publicly owned source plant, thermal boundary, candidate interface, route gallery, receiver field, measurement system, safety system, recovery system, site geometry, workforce, schedule and fiscal commitment are required to produce comparable full-scale transient evidence for all three `RHT-1` candidates without selecting one through the test apparatus?

The public boundary stops at a 0.90 m flange. Candidate-specific nozzle cassettes, route topologies, receivers and terminal systems remain separate, and no common downstream plenum is permitted.

Each rig uses a 2,000 kg source-vessel basis with a 1.50 m internal diameter, 1.76 m cylindrical tangent length and paired 2:1 ellipsoidal heads. Gross volume is approximately 3.994 m³; declared liquid occupies approximately 2.994 m³, leaving approximately 1.000 m³ of initial vapor space. A 3.0 MPa design, 4.5 MPa proof and 243–373 K planning envelope produces a 22.46 mm thin-wall screen and approximately 26.96 mm after declared allowances. The outfitted vessel screen is approximately 4.33 tonnes and the operating assembly approximately 7.83 tonnes. These are facility-sizing hypotheses, not code calculations or build release.

Sixteen independently metered 0.5 MW thermal zones provide 8 MW per rig and 48 MW across the federation. Three 80 m route bays per rig contain eight replaceable 10 m segments and twenty-one support stations. The resulting eighteen bays, 144 segments and fifty-four open, one-half and two-thirds restriction cassettes allow all three candidate path systems to be installed without hidden geometry changes.

Candidate aggregate path areas are approximately 0.2827 m² for one 0.60 m `RC-1` path, 0.3181 m² for two 0.45 m `RC-2` paths and 0.2886 m² for three 0.35 m `RC-3` paths. Their public-flange open-area ratios are approximately 44.4, 50.0 and 45.4 percent. Candidate route volumes are approximately 22.62, 25.45 and 23.09 m³. Eighteen serialized nozzle cassettes preserve candidate-specific contraction and branch behavior.

Six common receiver pads accept up to 60 tonnes of candidate equipment and establish 1.2 MN vertical, 1.2 MN axial and 1.5 MN-m overturning interface bases. Candidate-specific liquid-equivalent receiver volumes at 80 percent fill are approximately 4.68, 12.16 and 10.00 m³. Backpressure limits remain candidate-specific beneath a 0.30 MPa public ceiling.

Direct facility area is approximately 1.125 hectares per rig and 6.75 hectares across six rigs. Six non-overlapping 250 m planning exclusion circles require at least 117.81 hectares before terrain, access and consequence analysis. The inherited eighteen measurement functions preserve 504 channels and approximately 288.736 MB/s. The 300-second full-rate interval, six-hour one-percent tail, two native originals and 1.35 custody factor produce approximately 402.267 GB per event and 160.022 TB for the 306-event campaign with reserve. Root-sum-square combination is approximately 2.19 kg and expansion at \(k=2\) is approximately 4.38 kg against the inherited 5 kg limit. Physical calibration and event closure remain open.

Each rig has a passive mechanical or thermal safeing path, analog hardwired direct-current path and diverse programmable path with local stored energy. No pair may share a final element. Six rig-specific rescue and recovery packages remain separate physical acceptance objects. The source ladder begins with empty mechanical, hydrostatic, dry nitrogen, inert two-phase surrogate and cold-soak stages; 500 kg quarter-source, 2,000 kg full-source, destructive and independent-replication stages remain blocked by separate authority.

The program uses 372 mission positions over thirty months. Gross capital is USD 410M and annual support USD 72M/year.
